NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — FY2Q27 NVDA 財報亮眼，初步給予 FY28 70% 強勁成長指引

個股基本資料：

 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
NVDA FY2Q27 營收獲利優預期。財報公布時，股價盤後一度因 Q3 毛利率
受記憶體漲價承壓而走低，惟隨法說會揭露亞馬遜擴大採購，且提前給出  
FY2028 全年營收年增～70% 的強勁指引（顯著優於市場預期的 45%）後，
股價翻揚至上漲 4%。公司強調 70% 增速仍受限於產能（真實需求增長逾  
100%），一掃先前 AI 資本支出放緩疑慮。整體而言，短線雜音（毛利率壓
抑、循環融資疑慮）不改 Agentic AI 算力剛性需求的長線邏輯，隨著 Vera 
Rubin 於 8 月全面量產、Vera CPU（切入 2,000 億美元 TAM）與網路業務
持續擴大非 GPU 營收來源，加上聯手頂級機構籌集逾 5,000 億美元資本解
鎖生態系融資限制，算力霸主地位穩固，長線獲利動能可望延續。  
公司財報重點數據： 
NVDA FY2Q27 營收 962.2 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY+106%，連續四個季
度加速成長，優於市場預期的 920 億美元。Non-GAAP GM 為 75 %，符合
預期。Non-GAAP EPS 達 2.22 美元，優於市場預期。自由現金流達 213.4 
億美元，較上季明顯下滑，主因應收帳款及存貨等營運資本占用增加。季度
股利維持每股 0.25 美元，Q2 以庫藏股與股利合計返還股東 260 億美元，
剩餘庫藏股額度 990 億美元。 
業務部門表現： 
資料中心：營收 890 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY+117%，成長動能加速且
優於市場預期 。細分業務： 超大型雲端（ Hyperscale）營收 487 億美元
（QoQ+13%），優於市場預期，受惠 Blackwell Ultra 出貨持續強勁，並宣
布擴大與 AWS 合作，追加部署 200 萬顆 GPU；ACIE（涵蓋 AI 雲端、工
業、企業） 營收為  403 億美元（ QoQ+25%、YoY+138%），略低於預
期，成長動能 來自新雲端服務商（ Neo cloud ）產能擴充 。主權 AI 營收
QoQ+35%、YoY 逾 3 倍。 
邊緣運算：營收 72 億美元，QoQ+13%、YoY+27.5%，優於預期，主要由
受惠 Blackwell 工作站需求帶動，有效抵銷了消費性 PC 市場因記憶體及系
統漲價帶來的疲軟。 
公司展望： 
(1). 公 司 預 估FY3Q27 營收 1,080 億美元 (±2%) ， 優 於 市 場 預 期;GM 
74%(±50 bps) ，低於市場預期，主因記憶體零組件價格漲幅超出預期 ，預
計毛利率將於 Q4 打底後、隨 FY1Q28 執行漲價後逐步回升至 72~73%。 
(2). FY2027 全年  Non-GAAP OpEx 年增率上修至 low 50s ，稅率維持  
複委託報告 August  27, 2026

### Page 2

2
16%~18%。(3). Vera Rubin 8 月量產，預計佔 Q3 資料中心營收約 20%，
每百萬瓦吞吐量提升 30 倍。(4).初步預估 FY2028 全年營收成長約 70%（受
限於供應鏈產能 ，未受限需求高達  100%+），產能瓶頸預計至少持續至
FY2028 年底。(5). 獨立式 Vera CPU 正式量產，預計 FY2028 CPU 營收預
期翻倍（前 12 個月 Grace CPU 營收已破 50 億美元），確立其成為全球領
先伺服器 CPU 供應商。(6). 中國市場：中國資料中心運算(H200)營收比重
<1%，未來財測未納入任何中國資料中心運算營收。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
(1). 70%指引 vs. 100% 無限制需求的缺口：公司表示 70%為供給受限下可
確信兌現的數字，實際需求成長已達 100%，缺口主因在土地／電力／廠房
建置（Shell）等基礎建設籌建期偏長，而非單純晶片產能問題。(2). 推論市
占與客製化晶片防線： 強調 NVIDIA 為唯一橫跨全生命週期的全端平台，

Grok 3 LPX機架級系統已量產，定位為補強性產品，絕大多數資料中心仍以
Vera Rubin 平台為主。(3). 算力金融與資本：聯手 Apollo、BlackRock 等
六大機構籌集  >$500B 第三方資本，解決前沿 AI 實驗室融資限制；推行
Neo Cloud 營收分潤機制，提供最低營收擔保以獲取租金分成，開闢高複利
衍生收入。(4). Agentic AI 算力：推理調用需多消耗 15~100 倍算力，驅動
二次加速。平台完全兼容閉源（OpenAI）與開源模型（DeepSeek）。
公司簡介、競爭對手與公司自身價值：
NVIDIA 已成功由晶片巨頭轉型為全球  AI 算力工廠與基礎設施的生態系 霸
主。其護城河建立在  GPU、Vera CPU 、高階網路（ NVLink/Spectrum-
X）、 CUDA 軟體系與  Grok LPU 的「極端共同設計」（ Extreme Co -
design）。每吉瓦（GW）資料中心帶給 NVDA 的營收潛力已從 Hopper 時

代的 180 億美元大幅提升至 Vera Rubin 的 400 億美元。公司透過第三方資
本平台與 Neo Cloud 營收分潤，建立起客製化晶片（ASIC/ XPU）競爭對手
無法複製的商業模式與資金生態網，持續鞏固其在 Agentic AI 時代無可動搖
的平台優勢。

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Asia Pacific Semiconductors and Electronic Components &
Equipment
27 August 2026 Citi Research
2
•Custom ASIC Competition –   NVIDIA framed the ASIC competition around the economics and flexibility of the entire
“AI factory.” NVIDIA’s competitive advantage increasingly comes from integrating GPU compute, CPU, networking,
rack-scale architecture and software into one platform that can support multiple models and workloads across
different clouds. This changes the competitive discussion from whether a custom ASIC can outperform a GPU on a

specific task to whether hyperscalers can reproduce the same flexibility, software ecosystem and infrastructure
efficiency at scale. In our view, custom ASICs remain a credible competitive threat, particularly for stable and highly
optimized inference workloads, but NVIDIA's broader platform approach can defend its position even as the
accelerator market becomes. We would expect NVLink fusion more ready with AWS first in Trainium 4 generation, we

see Alchip would be the beneficiary as the major partner. We also see that Mediatek's early engagement with Nvidia
could be helpful for the company to generate more opportunity for second customer in ASIC in the longer term.
Companies Mentioned:
Alchip (3661.TW; NT$3870.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | Delta Electronics (2308.TW; NT$1770.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | Hon Hai
Precision (2317.TW; NT$252.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | Mediatek (2454.TW; NT$3865.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | NVIDIA Corp

(NVDA.O; US$209.66; 1; 26 Aug 26; 16:00) | Quanta Computer (2382.TW; NT$333.5; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | TSMC (2330.TW;
NT$2410.0; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30) | Wistron (3231.TW; NT$179.5; 1; 27 Aug 26; 13:30)

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between

participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 31 August 2026
Global Research
First Read
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Expanding partnership with Nvidia and Google
for AI computing platforms
What happened?
MediaTek announced the successful pricing of a US$3.9 billion overseas convertible
bond (CB) offering, with NVIDIA investing US$3.5 billion and the remainder subscribed
by Alphabet (Google) and leading global institutional investors, underscoring strong
confidence in the company’s AI strategy and expansion from custom AI silicon into

broader AI systems and data center platforms. Alongside the financing, MediaTek and
NVIDIA unveiled an expanded strategic partnership spanning AI infrastructure, local AI
computing, and automotive applications. A key highlight is MediaTek’s adoption of
NVIDIA’s NVLink Fusion platform, enabling customers to develop custom AI accelerators
that integrate with NVIDIA’s rack-scale AI factories. The collaboration will also extend
across multiple generations of AI PC chips, including RTX Spark and DGX Spark

platforms, as well as AI-powered software-defined vehicle solutions, further
strengthening MediaTek’s position across the AI value chain from edge to cloud.
Our view
We are optimistic about MediaTek's competitive positioning and opportunity in Cloud
and Edge AI. The partnership showcases Nvidia’s ongoing push to sell its NVLINK fusion
(including scale up photonics or electrical chiplet, C2C interconnect, and custom
NVHBM base die) into Cloud ASICs. Meanwhile, with compelling technology portfolio

of power efficient SoC, connectivity and advanced packaging, MediaTek could act as a
powerful partner of Nvidia to scale the AI solutions to the edge. Edge AI is only at the
beginning of development but could generate substantial upside in the next 5-10 years
across PC, automotive, robotics and others, which MediaTek and Nvidia could develop
industry leading solutions together. The expanded partnership with Nvidia could also
elevate MediaTek's technology capabilities for AI solutions. Separately for Google's TPU,

our view is the ASIC TAM is growing quickly in the next 2-3 years, and MediaTek should
expand its share from high 20% in 2027 to 40-45% with upside in 2028, based on TPU
die units. Google's effort in accelerating the new LLM development should support
robust demand for TPU v8t (training) designed by MediaTek in 2027. New generation
TPU like v10 coming in 2029-30 with chiplet structure may open up opportunity for
other ASIC vendors, but MediaTek should remain the main compute ASIC supplier given

the increasing integration complexity and recognize major portion of ASIC service
revenue, in our view.
Valuation: reiterate Key Call Buy and target of NT$6,500
Our target price is based on 27x 2027-28E PE. Maintain Key Call Buy rating.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Memory Innovation –  The Focus
from SEMICON Taiwan
SEMICON Taiwan highlighted memory value creation shifting
from bit scaling toward architecture-level innovation. OW on AP
Memory for emerging 3D WoW stacking. OW on Montage for
memory interface opportunities. OW on Macronix for SLC
NAND.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
For a broader discussion of these technologies, please refer to our Global Insight:
Innovating the Next-Generation Memory.
Memory is becoming a system-level differentiator: AI performance is increasingly
constrained by memory rather than computing. Longer context, agentic workflows,
and KV cache growth are raising the importance of bandwidth, capacity, and latency,
with tokens/$ and tokens/W becoming more relevant metrics. We see this

reinforcing the strategic importance of memory architecture as AI inference scales.
3D stacking is likely to be the future trend beyond today's 2.5D architecture:.
Samsung's zHBM and broader DRAM-on-logic concepts point to memory moving
from beside computinge toward vertically integrated architecture. This should
increase the importance of hybrid bonding, WoW integration, and thermal co-design.
We see AP Memory as a potential beneficiary of emerging 3D WoW stacking

opportunities.
Memory interfaces are becoming more strategic: As AI systems adopt
heterogeneous memory tiers across HBM, DRAM, CXL, SRAM and flash,
connectivity and control between computing and memory become complex. Google
mentioned that CXL is used to leverage old DDR4. We believe this raises the value
of memory interface solutions and reinforces our OW view on Montage.
(Continued in the following section)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Architecture-level innovations span memory interfaces 3D integration,
computing-near-memory, and new AI use cases on NAND.
Inference shifts Al bottleneck to memory bandwidth, capacity, and data
movement.
3D stacking is likely to be the next major architecture beyond today's 2.5D HBM,
supporting new opportunities for Top Pick AP Memory.

NAND moves up the Al memory hierarchy; creates new opportunities beyond
storage.
Memory and computing are converging through PIM, custom HBM, and near-
memory architectures.
September 1, 2026 06:03 PM GMT

### Page 2

Idea
2
Overview (cont'd)
NAND is gaining a more active role in Al systems: Fast NAND, high-IOPS SSDs, and HBF
are moving closer to computing for KV cache, model weights, and inference workloads,
broadening NAND's role beyond storage. We see this as supportive for higher-performance
NAND and potential incremental SLC NAND demand, reinforcing our OW view on
Macronix.

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles
AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)

Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost
of equity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) medium-term
growth rate of 16.0%, 3) payout ratio of 67%, 4) terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected consumer demand
n Faster-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Milder competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other

design houses
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected consumer demand
n Slower-than-expected IPD ramp and WoW packaging development
n Intensifying competition from partners (foundry, memory house, GPU vendor) or other
design houses
Montage Technology Co Ltd (6809.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n 8.4% CoE (1.2 beta, 3.0% risk-free rate, and 4.5% risk premium)
n 30% payout ratio
n 19.3% medium-term growth rate
n 4% terminal growth rate

These assumptions reflect cloud capex growth, DRAM interface technology migration, and
Montage's strong position in China's datacenter semi localization. We then apply an exchange

n Weaker-than-expected cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected DRAM interface technology migration
n Delay in new product launches
Montage Technology Co Ltd (688008.SS)

n 8.4% CoE (1.2 beta, 3.0% risk-free rate, and 4.5% risk premium)

n 30% payout ratio
n 19.3% medium-term growth rate

We believe these assumptions are justified, given the cloud capex growth, DRAM interface
technology migration, and Montage's strong position in China's datacenter semi localization.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Document 081: 260902_daiwa_NYPCB

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-02

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

2 September 2026

Spec upgrade to drive additional ABF substrate demand

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 7 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

Airtac (1590.TW)

3
Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,750 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 27.5x for 2H26-1H27E, which we derive from the mid range of
prior upcycle valuation 25-35x during 2021 in view of a strong upcycle with solid FA demand and ongoing share gains.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China

macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;
and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 6

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -7% MoM/+43%
YoY
Here we focus on AirTAC's monthly sales, which we believe
could be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• August sales came in at NT$3,708mn (-7% MoM/+43% YoY), with the
sequential decline mainly due to seasonality.
• By segment, shipments of textiles (+47% YoY), electronics (+36% YoY),
machine tool (+35% YoY) and battery (+30% YoY) outperformed during the
month.
• QTD revenue reached 66% of our estimate at NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41%

YoY) and 70% of consensus' at NT$11,015mn (-9% QoQ/+34% YoY).
Outlook:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue  tracking in line with our estimate.
• Management remains positive on its growth outlook driven by its share gains
and the policy-supported industry upcycle, which it expects to extend into
2027.
• Stay OW, as current valuation of 20x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its mid-
cycle average of 25x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

2026/09/02 投資速報

金融保險  
台新新光金 (2887.TT) 
合併帶動獲利跳增，但現金股利增幅恐受限

中立(維持評等)

加權指數報酬率 8.9 3.6 32.6 95.0 
個股股價報酬率 12.1 70.3 63.7 155.5 
季獲利走勢與年成長率

研究員/陳晏平 TINA.YP.CHEN@uni-psg.com

1H26 稅後淨利為 438 億元，成長 328%YOY，EPS 為 1.69 元。獲利
成長主要因:(1)台新銀核心收益皆逾兩成的成長 ， 該子公司獲利成
長 26%YOY 至 124 億元；(2)2025/7/24 完成合併新光金，新光銀、
元富證 1H26 合計額外貢獻 67 億元獲利，加上 2026 年接軌 IFRS 
17，壽險開始有穩定利差益，1H26 合併後壽險子公司的獲利為 225
億元。展望 2026~2027 年，由於:(1) 台新銀維持放款將高個位數
成長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修 2026 年淨利息收益至成長
19%YOY，並預期 2027 年將成長 5%YOY；(2) 台新銀財管商品銷售
動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期 2026 年整體淨手續費收益
將雙位數成長 ，2027 年個位數增加 ；(3) 台新銀資產品質穩定 ， 但
為新增放款 1%/1.5%法定提存，預期2026~2027 年淨提存將增加六
成至 30 億元 ；(4) 新壽接軌 IFRS 17 後擁穩定利差益 ， 另有80~100
億元的收購價格分攤數額外挹注，推估 2026 年將貢獻獲利逾 400
億元 ，2027 年亦將持續穩增；(5) 台股價量齊揚 ， 帶動證券子公司
獲利明顯增長，但同時也墊高基期。因此，在全年的合併效果下，
預估台新新光金 2026~2027 年稅後淨利為 805 億元、798 億元，成
長 116%、 衰退1%YOY， 扣除特別股股利25 億元後 ，EPS 為 3.11 元、
3.08 元 。 投資建議方面 ， 雖2026 年獲利明顯跳增 ， 且新壽1H26 已
累積 512 億元 OCI 資本利得亦可為配息來源，預期 2027 年現金股
利將優於 2026 年的 1 元水準 ， 不過2026 年獲利提升有部份是來自
無實際現金流入的 PPA，加上人壽能否上繳股利至金控尚不明確，
因此，在銀行、證券子公司較能上繳股利情況下，現金股利提升幅
度恐將受限。另以2027 年每股淨值 30 元、ROE 10~11%來看，目前
股價已逾 1.3xPBR，評價相對合理，故對台新新光金持「中立」投
資建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 台新銀放款將高個位數成 長，並上修淨利差擴大幅度，上修
2026年淨利息收益至成長19%YOY， 並預期2027年將成長5%YOY 
1H26底台新銀放款餘額為2.027兆元，成長11%YTD、12%YOY，
主要成長動能來自法人金融 、 加值型房屋貸款 、 個人無擔信用
貸款，分別增加15.5%、10%、15%YOY，一般房屋貸款則 小增
2%YOY。利差方面，雖高利差外幣放款成長動能強勁，1H26外
幣放款成長33%YOY，有提高資金運用效率 ，但受到台灣市場資
金緊俏影響，導致資金成本上揚，因此，2Q26存放利差為

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

1.53%，縮小6bpsQOQ、擴大14bpsYOY，淨利差亦同步 縮小
6bpsQOQ、擴大13bpsYOY至1.42%。不過因放款YOY持續成長、
淨利差YOY亦呈現擴大趨勢，1H26台新銀淨利息收益為200億
元，成長27%YOY。

圖一、台新銀利差趨勢

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，由於出口動能維持強勁，景氣持續成長，
台新銀維持原預期—法金放款雙位數增長 、個金放款穩健高
個位數成長 ， 預期台新銀2026年整體放款將高個位數成長 。但
因2027年即將與新光銀合併 ， 預期將有部分法金客戶重疊 ， 須
重新評估放款額度 ， 預期2027年放款成長性將會不如2026年。
利差方面 ， 雖近期台灣資金緊俏影響資金成本 ， 但因先前美國
降息刺激高利差外幣放款需求，在 1H26存放款結構調整優於
預期下，公司上修2026年淨利差擴大幅度至5~8bpsYOY(原預
期將擴大4~5bpsYOY)。 因此 ， 研究部上修台新銀2026年淨利息
收益2%至404億元，成長19%YOY，並預期2027年將續增5%YOY。

2. 台新銀財管商品銷售動能強勁，信用卡業務持續成長，預期
2026年整體淨手續費收益將雙位數成長，2027年個位數增加 
1H26台新銀整體淨手續費收益為110億元，成長32%YOY，主要
成長動能來自:(1)投資市場火熱刺激財富管理商品熱銷，整
體財富管理業務手續費收益成長32%YOY；(2)透過簽帳金額結
構改變(減少低/零手收)、控制權益支出，信用卡業務手續費
收入增加28%YOY；(3)隨企金放款增長，企金相關手續費增加
56%YOY。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

展望2026~2027年，因投資市場持續位在高檔，仍有利財富管
理商品銷售 ， 因此 ， 公司維持2026年台新銀整體淨手續費收益
將持續雙位數成長的預期，研究部推估將落在210~215億元水
準 ， 成長逾兩成 。 而在基期墊高下 ， 研究部預期2027年整體淨
手續費收益將個位數穩增。

3. 台新銀資產品質穩定，但為新增放款1%/1.5%法定提存，預期
2026~2027年淨提存將增加六成至30億元 
2026年以來無大額不良壞帳產生 ， 台新銀1H26底逾放比 、 呆帳
覆蓋率分別為0.13%、923%，資產品質不錯，些微優於產業平
均逾放比0.14%、呆帳覆蓋率940%。提存方面，雖針對新增逾
放的額外提存不多 ， 但因放款成長動能不弱 ， 而有新增放款的
1%或1.5%正常提存需求 ， 台新銀1H26淨呆帳提存為25億元 ，增
加112%YOY。

圖二、台新銀資產品質趨勢與同業平均水準比較

資料來源： 公司、統一投顧彙整

展望2026~2027年，目前資產品質穩定，暫無潛在大型不良放
款產生 ， 但因仍持續有新增放款的1%或1.5%正常提存， 公司預
期台新銀2026年信用成本將在10~15bps區間(維持不變)，較
2025年的10bps略增。研究部按公司預期的區間中上緣進行推
估，估計台新銀2026~2027年淨呆帳提存為30億元，較2025年
增加近六成。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

4. 新壽接軌IFRS 17後擁穩定利差益，另有80~100億元的收購價
格分攤數額外挹注，推估2026年將貢獻獲利逾400億元，2027
年持續穩增 
台新金與新光金合併案已在2025/7/24完成，台新金為存續公
司 ， 並更名為台新新光金 。 而旗下兩家投信子公司 、 兩家壽險
子公司、兩家證券子公司已分別在 2025/10/24、2026/1/1、
2026/4/6完成整併，並計畫2027/1/1完成兩家銀行子公司間
的整併。

其中 ， 兩家壽險子公司合併後 ， 台新人壽為存續公司 ， 但更名
為新光人壽。因2026年接軌IFRS 17，現階段壽險公司主要獲
利來源包括：保險服務結果、財務結果、其他營業結果。在保
險服務結果方面，1H26共釋出95億元CSM，加計RA釋出、實際
理賠及費用與預期的差異 ， 保險服務結果共140.5億元 。 此外 ，
美債殖利率仍位在高檔，新錢投資報酬率不差，且在
2025/7/24合併當下有針對投資資產進行重新評價，投資資產
規模因而明顯下降， 1H26避險前經常性收益率為 4.19%，較
2025年提升85bpsYOY。(若以同樣投資資產規模基礎，還原後
為3.43%，仍為提高趨勢) 另，受惠避險工具費用減降，避險
成本下滑至1.22%， 以及投資市場上漲 ， 多少帶動TPL投資部位
評價利益、處分利益增加，1H26避險後投資報酬率為3.73%。
(若加計OCI資本利得則為5.85%)負債成本則因接軌IFRS 17，
保單利息成本如預期降至2.3%，換算1H26利差益為1.43%，財
務結果貢獻179億元。再扣除其他營業結果、稅負等，1H26新
光人壽獲利為225億元。

展望2026~2027年，由於1H26新增351億元CSM(2026年目標為
700億元)，1H26底帳上CSM餘額仍有2936億元，若以1H26攤銷
率推估 ， 預期2026年將可釋出約200億元的CSM至損益表上。加
上公司預期經常性收益率可提升至 3.5%以上、保單成本亦有
望明顯降低 ， 往後可享有較穩定的利差益 ， 且持續有收購價格
分攤數(PPA)額外挹注(原先新壽AC項下的債券部位具有較大
額未實現損失 ， 在合併當下是採公允價值評價 ， 隨此些債券陸
續到期 ， 原本帳上未實現損失可望逐步攤銷出來 ， 並挹注至損
益表上，亦即為收購價格分攤數)，此次法說會公司揭露2026
年PPA將達80~100億元 ， 推估新壽2026年獲利將可逾400億元 。
而因新壽TPL部位佔比不高，且隨CSM穩定釋出，並持續有PPA
額外貢獻，預期2027年獲利將穩定成長。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

至於新光銀方面，1H26在淨利息收益穩增3%YOY、淨手續費收
益大增25%YOY、合併相關費用明顯減少、淨提存略有增加下，
獲利顯著大增168%YOY至48億元 。展望2026~2027年 ， 公司預期
2026年放款將個位數成長 、 淨利差維持穩定 ， 信用成本亦維持
原先預期的15~18bps，但淨手續費收益將雙位數成長(原預期
為中個位數增長)，加上營業費用正常化，因此，研究部上修
新光銀2026年獲利貢獻達96億元水準。

5. 台新新光金2027年現金股利將有望優於2026年，但人壽能否
上繳獲利至金控仍未可知，現金股利提升幅度恐不如預期 
資本適足率方面 ，旗下的台新銀、 新光銀1H26底整體資本適足
率、第一類資本適足率各為 13.7%、12.0%，以及14.24%、
12.41%，資本充足無虞。而合併後的新光人壽在 2026年接軌
TIS 時，已向主管機關申請在 125% ，而 1H26 底 約 莫 落 在
125~130%區間水準，在法定下限—100%之上。

股利配發率普遍落在35~50%區間水準 ， 另搭配些許股票股利 ，
整體股利配發率約莫在65~90%區間水準。但 因台新金為合併
新光金，台新新光金發行高達118.9億股的普通股來因應此次
合併案，使得台新新光金普通股股本較原先大幅膨脹九成 ， 因

配；(3)2026年以來股市顯著上漲，1H26新壽已累積512億元

### Page 1

Unimicron

Additional thoughts on the investigation 
incident of “products’ origin”

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 1,400 TWD | Price: 1,110 TWD

Investigation more likely related to non-substrate side 
Following our first take on the investigation incident (August 28), we lay out our further 
thoughts in this report. While the company does not disclose specifically which products 
or production sites are under investigation, supply chain checks indicate it is more likely 
to be related to the PCB (non-substrate), as the investigation may have taken place in 
the Hejiang plant (which focuses on PCB/HDI). 
Concerns on high-end ABF substrate look overstated 
Given that Unimicron’s high-end ABF substrate capacity is all located in Taiwan, we 
believe the issue of “mislabeling the products’ origin” may not be associated with such 
business. Rather, it is more likely associated with the PCB/HDI business, since the firm 
operates the factories in both China and Taiwan for production (and there could be some 
workload sharing in between, which involves the export/import).  
Fundamental impact minimal but share price likely to react 
PCB/HDI accounts for only 15% of the firm’s 2026 capex, and the business contributes 
no more than 10% of Unimicron’s profits, based on our estimates. We thus expect no 
significant impact on the company’s fundamental outlook, yet the share price may still 
react negatively on Monday, as the relevant issue (label of product origin) could be quite 
sensitive under the requirement/focus by US authorities and customers.  
Positive stance remains backed by S/D in ABF substrate 
On high-end ABF substrate, we do not think it makes sense for the firm to source from 
peers’ overseas capacity, given (1) Unimicron’s relative strength in product quality; and 
(2) the ongoing industry-wide supply shortage. Maintain Buy given its position as a 
major supplier (20+% share) in high-end substrate market.

Company Sector 
Electronics

Company Description 
Unimicron (3037 TT) was founded in 1990 and listed on 
the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 1998. The firm manufactures 
a wide range of PCB and IC substrates for consumer 
electronics, industrial, and automotive. It is also the leading 
ABF supplier globally. The company merged with Phoenix 
Precision Technology (PPT, delisted) in 2009 and expanded 
its exposure in the IC substrate business. UMC is the 
biggest shareholder in Unimicron with a 13.3% stake.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Unimicron in view of strong 
demand from AI accelerators (especially ASICs), which we 
expect to boost the utilization rate of the ABF substrate 
business from 2H26, in turn bolstering the company's 
margin profile and profitability going into 2027.

Stock Data  
Price to Book Value 13.0x

### Page 3

Price objective basis & risk 
Unimicron (UMCRF) 
Our PO of NT$1,400 is based on 35x 2H27-1H28E, which is roughly 1 standard deviation 
above the historical average of 20x since 2017. In our view, the multiple is appropriate, 
as we expect Unimicron's earnings to be supported by its high-end technology offerings, 
where end market demand seems more resilient.

Upside risks are (1) tighter ABF supply, (2) greater PC and regular server demand, and (3) 
lower competition pressure.

Downside risks are (1) greater ABF expansion from peers, (2) lower ASP content increase 
at AI server, and (3) bigger drop in the demand of consumer electronic products.

壹、投 資 建 議

一. 先進製程、先進封裝需求暢旺帶動載具出貨  
隨著製程持續微縮，EUV 光罩層數增加，加上先進製程產能持續
擴充，有助光罩及晶圓載具需求成長。公司之載具解決方案：包
括 EUV Pod、RSP 150、FOUP、FOSB 等，未來將持續受惠半導體
產能擴充及製程微縮之載具需求。先進封裝部分，隨著各大半導
體廠積極擴充先進封裝產能，家登在先進封裝載具認證順利，尤
其是 FOPLP 在尺寸規格逐步統一後，2027 年將翻倍放量。

二.  2026 年下半年營收逐季成長，預估全年 EPS 16.52 元 
展望 2026 年，受惠於 AI 帶動之先進製程需求，大客戶於海內外
積極擴增產能，帶動 EUV Pod 及 Diffusion FOUP 放量成長，加上
FOUP 於大中華區之拉貨將由去年谷底翻揚 ， 加上先進封裝營收開
始明顯貢獻 ， 在主要產品線營收成長強勁下 ， 2026 年營收將創高。
預估 2 0 2 6 年營收 9 7 . 3 9 億( Y o Y + 3 2 . 5 6 % )，稅後淨利 1 5 . 8 8 億
(YoY+75.41%)，EPS 16.52 元。

三.  2027 年營收獲利可望創高，預估 EPS 20.99 元 
展望 2027 年 ， AI 帶動之半導體晶片需求 ， 將帶動 EUV Pod 及 FOUP
出貨提升，而大陸市場 2025 年 FOUP 庫存調整，使 2025 年 FOUP
出貨量較 2024 年腰斬，不過隨著中國半導體在地化趨勢加上產品
技術克服將帶動客戶重啟 FOUP 拉貨，家登於全球 FOUP 市占
20~30%，未來可望逐步上升至 5 成。家登亦從跨至先進封裝，因
應 CoWoS 、CoPoS 、FOPLP 等各種先進封裝型式提供客製化載具，
目前大尺寸 FOPLP 規格逐步統一，公司產品已過客戶認證，2026
年營收翻倍 ， 2027 年營收可望再翻倍 。 預估家登 2027 年營收 127.46
億(YoY+30.87%)，稅後淨利 20.18 億(YoY+27.09%)，EPS 20.99 元。

四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 630 元  
家登專注於半導體之光罩／晶圓載具，產品於市場具高市占率，
受惠於大客戶在先進製程領域技術領先，公司亦將隨先進製程產
能擴充成長 ， 加上先進封裝載具放量成為新成長動能 。 考量到 1)AI
帶動之先進製程需求強勁 ， 大客戶積極擴產、 2)公司逐步從先進製
程跨入先進封裝、記憶體載具、3) 今明年營收獲利持續創高，參
考 2027 年 EPS 給予 30 倍本益比，給予目標價 630 元，建議買進。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
家登精密成立於 1998 年 ， 原從事塑膠外殼模具 CNC 加工 ， 在 2000
年切入半導體前段製程設備、零件領域，並在 2001 年正式通過台
積電認證，成為台積電台灣第一家黃光微影製程用零組件的本土
供應商，是國內半導體前段黃光微影製程光罩保護、傳送及儲存
解決方案的領導廠，其 EUV 光罩盒全球市占率達八成以上。公司
2020 年正式跨足航太領域，將精密加工核心技術延伸至不同產
業，擴大應用及版圖。公司兩大事業群：半導體事業及航太事業，
半導體產品包括光罩傳載、晶圓傳載、先進封裝傳載、機台設備。
航太產業部份 ， 公司取得 AS9100D 證書 ， 積極布局上下游供應鏈，
已開始向美系航太客戶出貨，主要供應航太操縱控制器的液壓管
件 (Barrel) 。2025 年產品營收比重為光罩載具占 42% 、晶圓載具占
25%，機台設備占 16%，半導體原料耗材占 3%，其他(清洗服務、
零件等)占 14% 。 公司半導體主要客戶包括半導體代工廠 、 封測廠、
記憶體廠、設備商。

二.2026 年營運概況及 2027 年展望 
1. 先進製程、先進封裝需求暢旺帶動載具出貨 
隨著製程持續微縮，EUV 光罩層數增加，7nm(N7+) EUV 光罩層數
約 4~5 層，5nm 增加至 10~15 層，3nm 增加至 20~25 層，儘管 2nm
光罩層數約持 20 層左右，然隨著 2、3nm 製程產能持續擴充，有
助光罩及晶圓載具需求成長。家登在高階光罩具技術領先優勢，
目前全球市占約 85% ，公司之載具解決方案：包括 EUV Pod、RSP 
150、FOUP、FOSB 等，而家登亦開發出 High NA EUV Pod，成為
第一家通過 ASML 認證之供應商，未來將持續受惠半導體產能擴
充及製程微縮之載具需求。先進封裝部分，隨著各大半導體廠積

2. 2Q26 本業業外均貢獻，EPS 5.53 元

億(QoQ+116.83%、YoY+1156.69%)，EPS 5.53 元。

3. 2026 年下半年營收逐季成長，預估全年 EPS 16.52 元 
展望 2026 年，受惠於 AI 帶動之先進製程需求，大客戶於海內外

FOUP 於大中華區之拉貨將由去年谷底翻揚，FOUP 滲透率持續提
升，加上先進封裝營收開始明顯貢獻，截至今年前 7 月，EUV Pod
營收已達 2025 全年的 82%，FOUP 營收已達 2025 全年 60%，先進
封裝載具已達 2025 全年 2.44 倍 ， 在主要產品線營收均將超過 2025
年下，2026 年營收將創高，毛利率在高階產品比重提升下將維持
高 檔 水 準 。 預 估 2026 年 營 收 97.39 億 (YoY+32.56%)， 毛 利 率
43.47%，稅後淨利 15.88 億(YoY+75.41%)，EPS 16.52 元。

展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之半導體晶片需求，包括先進製程及記憶體

EUV Pod 及 Diffusion FOUP 需求，而公司 High NA EUV Pod 已小量

隨著中國半導體在地化趨勢加上產品技術克服帶動客戶重啟
FOUP 及 FOSB 拉貨，家登於全球 FOUP 市占 20~30% ，未來可望逐

從前段製程跨至先進封裝，因應 CoWoS 、CoPoS 、FOPLP 等各種先

入障礙」、「高毛利市場」及「精密加工」為產品佈局方向，加

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一.  2Q26 本業業外均貢獻，EPS 5.53 元 
2Q26 營收 23.15 億元(QoQ+24.11%、YoY+35.37%)，毛利率升至
43.71% ， 業外受益於金融資產評價利益及匯兑利益貢獻 2.89 億元 ，
稅後淨利 5.32 億(QoQ+116.83%、YoY+1156.69%)，EPS 5.53 元。

二. 2026 年下半年營收逐季成長，預估全年 EPS 16.52 元 
展望 2026 年，在主要產品線營收均將超過 2025 年下，2026 年營
收將創高，毛利率在高階產品比重提升下將維持高檔水準。預估
2026 年營收 97.39 億(YoY+32.56%) ， 毛利率 43.47% ， 稅後淨利 15.88
億(YoY+75.41%)，EPS 16.52 元。

三. 2027 年營收獲利可望創高，預估 EPS 20.99 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之半導體晶片需求，包括先進製程及記憶體
需求放量，帶動 EUV Pod 及 Diffusion Foup 需求，家登在半導體亦
從前段製程跨至先進封裝，提供客製化載具，公司產品已過客戶
認證，2026 年營收翻倍，2027 年營收可望再翻倍。預估家登 2027
年營收 127.46 億(YoY+30.87%)，毛利率 41.61%，稅後淨利 20.18
億(YoY+27.09%)，EPS 20.99 元。

股價相對大盤走勢

大銀微系統 (4576 TT)  Buy

先進封裝、矽光子相關商機 2H26 持續擴大，評等為 Buy。 
投資建議 ：受惠於半導體前段製程、 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單
挹注，以及矽光子檢測設備相關訂單數量自 07/2026 開始放大，
微米及奈米級定位系統 2H26 營收成長性可期，加上精密運動
及控制元件需求亦持續回溫，故群益的投資建議為 Buy 。  
2Q26 營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，但受到匯兌損失影響，稅後淨利
QoQ-0.73% ：2Q26 營收 10.23 億元， QoQ+19.70% ，除精密運動
及控制元件營收動能續強外，微米及奈米級定位系統需求亦再
增溫，相關營收 5.34 億元， QoQ+17.20% ，雖然毛利率受到匯率
影響而降低至 37.10% ，但營業利益 QoQ+28.89% ，惟業外表現
不及 1Q26(1Q26 認列 0.10 億元匯兌利益， 2Q26 認列 0.37 億元
匯兌損失 )，稅後淨利 1.15 億元， QoQ-0.73% 。  
2H26 營收展望佳： 展望 2H26 ，微米及奈米級定位系統營收成
長性仍可期，目前訂單能見度達 5~6 個月，動能主要來自半導
體相關產品，原因有二， (1) 由於前段製程設備大廠訂單穩定挹
注，此部份商機主要來自台灣晶圓代工廠持續擴充先進製程，
(2) 台灣設備供應商 CoWoS 測試設備相關訂單數量持續增加，
且 CoPoS 、FOPLP 測試設備相關產品已送樣至客戶認證。此外，
矽光子檢測設備相關訂單自 04/2026 開始出貨給台灣半導體設
備供應商，且數量自 07/2026 開始放大， 07~08/2026 達數百台，
客戶並於 08/2026 兩度追加訂單，訂單能見度達 3~4 季。  
為因應微米及奈米級定位系統需求增溫，大銀微陸續啟動兩階
段擴產作業，第 1 階段已於 06/2026 完成，產能擴大約 20% ，
第 2 階段則預計於 2026 年年底至 2027 年年初之間完成，屆時
產能將再擴大 20% ，均為營收持續成長的後盾。  
至於精密運動及控制元件 2H26 營收展望亦佳，包括中國、東南
亞 (新加坡、印度等 )等地 PCB 鑽孔機、成型機設備供應商相關
需求自 4Q25 開始回溫，目前訂單能見度為 2.5~3 個月，營收成
長性較佳的產品包括線性馬達、伺服馬達、驅動器等。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 3 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略而言，大銀微成立 「氣候變遷風險小
組」，由董事長主持，主導全公司減碳因應方針及短、中、長期減碳策略，
依循氣候相關財務揭露建議書 (TCFD) 架構以進行氣候風險及機會評估，並
透過暴露程度與脆弱度矩陣研擬調適行動計畫，分別進行氣候變遷風險分
析評估潛在財務衝擊。  
再就企業溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫而言，大銀微委託外部輔導團
隊協助教導 ISO 14064 溫室氣體盤查類別 3~類別 5 的執行項目，藉由盤查
間接溫室氣體所產生的碳排放數據，重整與規劃制定相關管理措施，使其降
低溫室氣體排放。至於大銀微持續進行節能減碳改善規劃方案則主要有五，
(1) 安裝節能智慧電表，結合節能 e 化系統管控電力使用，找出節能方案，
(2) 空壓機系統更換變頻空壓機或租賃馬力數較低的空氣用量， (3) 汰換冰水
機，選擇高效能的變頻冰水節能設備， (4) 分析找尋替代低碳及可回收材料，
並應用於產品循環經濟設計上， (5) 培養員工節能減碳的理念並養成節約用
電的好習慣。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

精測 (6510 TT)  Buy

精測受惠 HPC 測試需求， 2H26 營運優於 1H26，給 Buy 的
投資評等。  
投資建議 ：精測受惠於 HPC 測試需求， 3Q26 營運可望持續季
增、 2027 年營運也將優於 2026 年，給 Buy 的投資評等。  
輝達入股聯發科，有助精測短期評價 ：聯發科 (2454 TT) 持股翔
發投資 100% ，翔發投資持股精測約 0.5% 。輝達 (NVDA US) 認
購聯發科的 ECB 約 35 億美元，有助聯發科 \精測的短期評價與
中長期營運。  
精測有 TPU 與 AI5 商機 ：精測表示，美系客戶的 TPU 供應商
維持既有架構，預期在  TPU8 專案中將扮演一定角色，不過目
前尚未正式發包，相關專案預計於 2H26 啟動。此外，針對另一
客戶的  AI5 專案，依客戶規劃時程，預計 06/2026 進行工程卡
交付，並預計於 2026 年底至 2027 年初進入量產。  
精測新產能持續開出 ：2026 年營運逐季成長趨勢明確， 3Q26 營
收有機會雙位數季增， 08/2026 、 03/2027 會有比較大的產能開
出。目前交期約 5~8 周，新產能客戶驗證時間約需一季， 2027
年營運健康成長。  
精測 3Q26 毛利率可能季減 ：3Q26 毛利率區間為 50~55% ，因新
產品一開始會有學習曲線需爬升，後段測試 \老化測試的毛利率
也較低。估 2H26 每季費用約 3.8~4.2 億元，全年費用率約
20~25% 。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
在環境保護方面，中華精測於 2022 年開始自主性導入溫室氣體盤查作業，
除了計算碳使用總量外，也了解如何節能及尋找替代能源，並推動各項環保
節能減碳措施、減少溫室氣體排放量等，以期減緩與調適氣候變遷所帶來之
營運衝擊。  
在社會責任方面，中華精測遵守相關法規及國際人權公約，持續營造友善、
平等、自由的工作環境，對於提升員工福祉不遺餘力，且不定期舉辦家庭活
動及獎勵同仁結婚、生育、旅遊津貼等員工福利。  
在公司治理營運績效方面，中華精測連續兩年於公司治理評鑑排名前 5%，
並於 2022 年榮獲上櫃公司治理評鑑  Top5% 之佳績，秉持誠信正直原則，定
期宣導及教育訓練內線交易、誠信廉潔等法律知識，確保公司員工遵守誠信
經營守則之落實。  
公司簡介：  
中華精測科技股份有限公司 (6510 TT) 設立於 08/2005 ，總部及生產工廠皆位
於桃園平鎮，為中華電信所屬關係企業，前身為中華電信研究所內部之高速
PCB 團隊， 2001 年起開始嘗試研發設計測試載板 (Load board) 及探針卡載板
(Probe PCB) 。  
中華精測主要從事晶圓測試板、 IC 測試板、技術服務與其他等業務，提供
半導體產業測試所需的介面板服務，用於晶圓測試之探針卡專用印刷電路
板 (Probe PCB) ，及用於 IC 測試之載版 (Load Board) 。  
中華電信 (2412 TT) 持股中華投資公司約 89.0% ；中華投資持股精測約
33.6% ；聯發科 (2454 TT) 於 2015 年，透過旗下翔發投資公司入股中華精測，
目前持股約 0.5% 。  
精測的台灣同業包含旺矽 (6223 TT) 、穎崴 (6515 TT) 、雍智科技 (6683 TT)…
等。精測的客戶包含美系 HPC 公司、台系 IC 設計公司、美系 IC 設計公司，
台系晶圓代工廠 … 等。  
營收占比 _產品別：  
晶圓測試卡包含 PCB 與整組探針卡。

資料來源：精測法說會簡報。

3 群益投顧 
營收占比 _產品應用別：

資料來源：精測法說會簡報。  
精測 3Q26 毛利率可能季減 ：  
3Q26 毛利率區間為 50~55% ，因新產品一開始會有學習曲線需爬升，後段測
試 \老化測試的毛利率也較低。估 2H26 每季費用約 3.8~4.2 億元，全年費用
率約 20~25% 。  
精測新產能持續開出 ：  
2026 年營運逐季成長趨勢明確， 3Q26 營收有機會雙位數季增， 08/2026 、
03/2027 會有比較大的產能開出。目前交期約 5~8 周，新產能客戶驗證時間
約需一季。 2027 年營運健康成長。  
精測有發 CB，三廠廠能預估 4Q28 開出：  
精測 07/29 重訊決議發行 CB，預計募資 20 億元，主要用於新廠的工程款，
其餘用於營運、新材料、新產品的研發。三廠預計 4Q25 開始動工、 4Q28 貢
獻營收，新產能是舊產能的 100% 以上。  
其他補充：  
美系車用客戶的 AI 晶片，精測目前有在送樣測試產品，驗證進度 85% 。  
2026 年 CAPEX 從 3.5 億元提高到 5.0 億元，用在擴充產能、承租廠房； 2026
全年折舊金額約 4.1 億元。  
TPU 改用 EMIB-T 封裝，之後還是要回台灣做測試，對精測影響不大。原物
料上漲，精測會反映給客戶，但精測不會因為供需吃緊而漲價。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

南俊(6584) 
建議：維持中立 
結論：2H26 通用 Server 缺料紓緩，加上 AI Server 新
平台挹注，營運將回復成長，惟 PER 仍偏高

一、2Q26 毛利率、營益率優於預期，加上業外淨支出
0.21 億元 ， 獲利達成率113%， 單季稅後EPS 3.03 元，
累計 1H26 稅後 EPS 6.25 元 
南俊 2Q26 財報 ， 營收8.46 億元 、-2%QoQ、+41%YoY，
營收季衰退 ， 主要係因通用Server 缺料致客戶拉貨放緩，
毛利率 41.81%(原 41.14%)、+0.26ppts QoQ、+9.31ppts 
YoY，營收季衰退，但毛利率仍逆勢上揚，主要係因產
線及產能越南廠比重攀升至 20%所致，採越南當地鋼廠
材料 ， 致成本具優勢 ， 維持毛利率處高檔 ， 營業利益2.51
億元、-2%QoQ、+159%YoY，達成率 103%，營益率
29.71%(原28.86%)、+0.25ppts QoQ、+13.54ppts YoY，
業外淨收入 0.21 億元，其中匯兌收益 5.773M 元、其他
收入 0.1 億元，稅前淨利 2.73 億元、-7%QoQ、
+490%YoY， 稅後淨利2.19 億元 、-7%QoQ、-2516%YoY，
獲利達成率 113%， 單季稅後EPS 3.03 元(原估 2.67 元)。
累計 1H26 營收 17.13 億元、+56%YoY，營業利益 5.07
億元、+199%YoY，稅前淨利 5.67 億元、+342%YoY，
稅後淨利 4.54 億元、+517%YoY，稅後 EPS 6.25 元。

二、2Q26 雲端 Server 受累通用 Server 缺料，客戶拉
貨放緩，拖累營收季衰退，僅廚櫃/系統櫃、醫療/工業等
應用逆勢季成長 
2Q26 營收 8.46 億元、-2%QoQ、+41%YoY，營收比重
分別是雲端 Server 占 69%、辦公傢俱占 9%、工具櫃用
占 7%、櫥櫃/系統櫃占 6%、醫療/工業占 7%、家電占
2%。其中季增率表現，櫥櫃/系統櫃、醫療/工業等季成
長外，餘均季衰退；年增率表現，雲端 Server、櫥櫃/
系統櫃、醫療/工業等達雙位數的年成長，工具櫃、家電
及辦公傢俱等年衰退雙位數 。 雲端Server 季增率衰退，
主要係通用 Server 因缺料致客戶拉貨放緩，致整體
Server 比重下滑。 
累計 1H26 營收+56%YoY， 營收比重分別是雲端Server
占約 71%、+116%YoY，工具櫃占 7%、-16%YoY，家
電用占 3%、-53%YoY， 廚櫃/系統櫃占 5%、+11%YoY，
辦公傢俱占9%、 持平YoY， 醫療/工業占 5%、+56%YoY，
其中雲端 Server、廚櫃/系統櫃、醫療/工業等年增率均
有雙位數以上。  
1H26 雲端 Server 占總營收比重 71%，其中泛 AI(包括
RTX、L40S…等)占總營收比重約 15~20%，GB 系統占
總營收比重僅 5%，餘主要是通用 Server 為主。目前越
南廠稼動率滿載，台灣廠稼動率約 6-7 成。 
三、展望 3Q26，通用 Server 缺料供應將紓解，加上
AI Server 新平台挹注 ， 將推升營收回復季成長 ， 單季稅
後 EPS 3.14 元 
展望 3Q26，通用 Server 缺料供應將紓解，配合新機
(Birch Stream)拉貨，加上 AI Server 的 VR 平台開始少
量出貨，ASIC 平台(氣冷方案)9 月可量產出貨，2Q26
是營運相對低檔，2H26 將回復成長趨勢。7 月營收 2.8
億元、-2%MoM、+33%YoY，季營收達成率 32%，低於
預期 ， 累計營收+52%YoY。 投顧維持預估3Q26 營收8.82
億元、+4%QoQ、+42%YoY，毛利率 42.9%、
+1.09pptsQoQ、+6.37pptsYoY，營益率 31.05%、
+1.34pptsQoQ、+9.33pptsYoY，稅後淨利 2.27 億元、
+4%QoQ、+74%YoY，稅後 EPS 3.14 元。 
四、展望 2026 年，雲端 Server 仍是主要成長動能，通
用 Server 缺料紓料，加上 NV VR 平台及 ASIC 平台等
AI Server 挹注，預估估全年營收+48%YoY，稅後 EPS 
13.01 元 
展望2026 年 ， 成長主要動能仍在雲端Server 產品應用，
過去 NVIDIA GB 平台自 2024 年即開始取得 NVIDIA 
RVL，此部分僅針對設計及產線，實際上各家CSP 滑軌
的需求有些不同，包括防呆更高、承重更高，故 ODM
仍會測試產品，通過後才會在 ODM 的供應名單中，由
CSP 正式採用。而1H26 GB 應用占營收比重仍僅約 5%，
主要係南俊產品 NPI 速度比同業慢，致市佔率少拿了很
多，預期進入下一世代 VR，已開始小量出貨，且VR 客

戶較 GB 客戶多出約 3~4 家，南俊擬透過更多的 VR 終

五、維持中立理由 
投顧預估 2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.01 元及 18.84 元，
目前股價 620 元，PER 分別為 48X 及 33X 仍舊相當高，
南俊自去年至今，AI Server 表現挹注不如預期，惟受惠
通用 Server 需求強勁，雲端 Server 占比已逾 7 成，AI 
Server 可望在 2H26 顯著挹注 ， 致營收可望季季增 ，4Q26 
AI Server 是否穩定出量，成為影響 2027 年營運是否顯
著成長的重要關鍵，惟 2027 年 PER 仍高達 33X，未顯
著偏低，故維持投資評等中立，給予目標價 600 元。

復盛為全球最大高爾夫球具及球頭代工生產廠商 ：復盛為全球最大高爾夫球具及球
頭代工生產廠商，市佔率超過4 成，公司營運結構及產品包括高爾夫球桿頭、球桿
頭配件、碳桿、民盛、航太、棒壘球棒與美國廠區，營收比重上，高爾夫球相關營
收占比約 80%，其餘約 20%，客戶上，高爾夫球部分國際主要品牌廠商如
Callaway、T aylormade、Titleist、PING、HONMA 等皆為公司主要客戶，國內
主要競爭廠商包括大田、明安、鉅明等。 
天氣持續利好，今年前六月美國地區下場打球次數YoY 達 4.2%：由於高爾夫球運
動極度依賴天氣，相較去年，今年前六個月在天氣普遍不錯下，推動下場打球次數
YoY 達 4.2%，為高球產業球具等需求帶來買氣支撐。另在美國地區高爾夫球具零
售市場表現部分，2Q26，高爾夫球具銷值 QoQ 及 YoY 分別為+72.1%及+3.9%，
庫存額部分，QoQ 及 YoY 分別為+12.9%及+3.9%，存貨/銷售值比 1.24X(1Q26 
1.89X、4Q25 1.71X)，產業庫存仍在相對健康水位。  
費用率及單季稅率較高，2Q26 獲利表現略低於預期：復盛 2Q 營收 80.7 億元(-
4.7%QoQ、+22.9%YoY)，毛利率 25.7%，因費用率 10.1%較預期 9.2%高，加上
單季稅率 24.8%，亦高於預期 20%下，稅後淨利 8.56 億元 (-11.8%QoQ、
+170.7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.14 元，低於原預期之 6.74 元。 
3Q 淡季不淡，4Q 旺季依舊旺：3Q 雖為傳統淡季，但因去年基期低及出貨狀況不
錯，預估 3Q 營收為 77.8 億元(-3.6%QoQ、+32%YoY)，淡季不淡，毛利率因產
品組合關 係，預 估將微 幅 下滑至25%， 稅後淨 利8.44 億元 (-1.4%QoQ 、
+73.7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.05 元，以目前出貨規劃，4Q 進入旺季下，營收將跳升
至 94.8 億元(+21.9%QoQ、+7.2%YoY)，挑戰歷史新高，毛利率因逢新品出貨高
峰 ， 預估可跳至26.1%， 稅後淨利11.4 億元(+35.2%QoQ、+3.7%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
8.18 元。  
其他子公司部分：(1) 進行冰上曲棍球、越野摩托車、棒球、滑雪等運動鞋靴與頭
盔及護具之代工之民盛：隨大客戶出貨恢復正常下，2H 營收將明顯優於 1H，目
前生產及出貨皆回穩，2027 年營運預期將回復成長；(2) 手工具廠伯鑫：接單穩
定，訂單穩健成長中；(3) 專精汽車鋁合金精密鍛造之力野：新訂單將挹注營收成
長，惟獲利因利潤率較低下，預期 2027 年與 2026 年將呈持平狀；(4) 從事精密

金屬成型與表面處理技術之中山奧博： 在復盛高爾夫球事業表現穩健下， 中山奧博
營運亦穩定，每年營收約25-30 億元，獲利約4-5 億元；(5) 專攻航太精密零件之
嘉華盛：接單狀況不錯，今明兩年展望佳且開始獲利，此外，復盛運用其在精密鑄
造與鍛造的技術優勢，跨足 AI 散熱模組領域，其相關零組件已成功切入數家中國
一線廠商供應鏈，惟目前處發展初期，營收貢獻度與穩定度需再觀察一段時間。

28.38 元)。2027 年營收 351.6 億元(+4%YoY)，毛利率 25.4%，稅後淨利 41.2 億
元(+8.1%YoY)，稅後 EPS 29.56 元。

### Page 1

楊凱云 
(886-2)2563-1666 
pineapple.yang@firstsec.com.tw

兆聯實業(6944) 
買進, TP 1,080 (潛在報酬 52.33%)  
買進 
Price(09/02):709 
Target Price：1080

2Q26 營收獲利續創歷史新高，主要受惠於在手訂單攀升及美國
P2 廠工程進度優於預期，加上記憶體客戶擴廠需求較為急迫，部
分專案利潤率優於平均水準，使2Q26毛利率再次優於預期。展望
2026 年，隨美國 P2 廠進入主體工程階段，營收動能將自 2Q26
起加速，加上國內晶圓廠、美國P3廠、記憶體及載板廠接續動工，
預估全年營收將逐季走揚。展望後市，目前在手訂單金額約 380
億元，受惠主要客戶持續加速赴美擴廠，加上記憶體、載板及矽晶
圓產業擴產需求延續，全年新接單金額有望超越 2025年，能見度
達 1.5~2 年。獲利方面，隨海外高單價訂單及高毛利工程占比提
升，整體毛利率有望維持27~28%的高檔水準，惟公司將提撥獲利
19%作為員工酬勞，預估費用率將小增 0.3ppt 至 4.49%。綜合以
上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 41.22、53.94 元，考量(1)
公司為國內廢水回收系統工程龍頭，且工程實績及低費用率優勢、
(2)主要客戶加速赴美擴廠，訂單需求強勁、(3) 營運規模擴大及學
習曲線效益顯現，獲利能力持續提升，以中性 20倍本益比評價，
目標價為 1080元，投資建議為買進。 
 近期投資評等  美國 P2工程加速推進，2Q26營收獲利續創高 
2Q26 營收 50.8 億元，YoY+36.14%，QoQ+7.94%，稅後損
益 8.72 億元，YoY+70.76%，QoQ+12.82%，EPS 8.75元(以
追溯後新股本計算 )，優於研究部預期。營收結構 : 工程收入
77%、勞務收入 23%。第二季營收創歷史新高，主要受惠在
手訂單維持高檔及美國 P2 廠工程加速推進，帶動工程收入
YoY+35%、QoQ+2.45%；此外，勞務收入自3Q25 導入新耗
材 MBR 後，效益逐步顯現，2Q26 勞務收入 YoY+40.39%、
QoQ+32.14% 。 獲 利 方 面 ，2Q26 毛 利 率28.44% ，
YoY+7.45ppt，QoQ+1.3ppt，續創歷史新高，主要來自 : (1)
記憶體等高毛利訂單挹注、 (2)海外工程效率優於預期，費用
及成本管控效益提升、(3)美國 P2 廠接單及發包時，新台幣兌
美元匯率相對強勢，隨後匯率貶至 31 元，在收入與成本認列
時點差異下，有助於營收認列及獲利提升。

 主要客戶加速赴美擴廠，在手訂單逾 380 億元

現金股利(去年) 14 進，海外在手訂單比重下滑至 25%，若加計 7/21 公告取得美
國 P3 廠廢水處理系統工程，整體在手訂單上看380 億元，海
外比重亦將提升至 40%。在主要客戶持續擴大海外產能下，
2026 年資本支出預估達 520~560 億美元，YoY+47~56%，潛
在訂單包含美國 P4 廠、屏東、嘉義、中科FAB 廠及 AP 廠。
另一方面，矽晶圓、載板及記憶體客戶因接單動能強勁，今明
年均有擴廠規劃，公司目前已取得南亞科、新興、美光等相關
訂單，其餘潛在訂單包含美光新加坡廠、合晶等，預期全年新
增訂單金額有望超越 2025 年。獲利方面，憑藉豐富的工程實
績、持續擴大的營運規模與學習曲線效應，工程毛利率已穩定
提升至 25%以上，且因記憶體客擴廠需求急迫，工期縮短有
助於提高專案執行效率，帶動 1H26 工程毛利率達 27%，預
計 2H26 工程毛利率可維持 2Q26 水準；同時，隨採購規模擴
大，服務類毛利率亦達 30%，帶動整體獲利顯著成長。費用
方面，公司將提撥獲利 19%作為員工酬勞，預估費用率將小
增至 4.49%，YoY+0.3ppt。展望後市，主要客戶加速赴美擴
廠，且因當地建造與人力成本為台灣的 5~7 倍，高價訂單將
成為主要成長動能；此外，矽晶圓、載板、記憶體產業同樣進
入擴產週期，廠務需求持續增加，考量人力配置及專案執行效
率，公司將採擇優接單策略，持續優化訂單結構與獲利表現。
另公司以統包商角色提供設備與工程服務，清關與運費由客戶
吸收，關稅與運價衝擊有限；匯率方面，海外訂單成本結構約
80%為美元、20%為台幣，具天然避險效果，整體匯率風險尚
屬可控。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 232.37 億元，
YoY+37.39%，稅後損益 41.09 億元，YoY+73.27%，EPS 
41.22 元；2027 年營收 309.27 億元，YoY+33.09%，稅後損
益 53.77 億元，YoY+30.87%，EPS 53.94 元。

 結論與建議 
2Q26 營收獲利續創歷史新高，主要受惠於在手訂單攀升及美
國 P2 廠工程進度優於預期，加上記憶體客戶擴廠需求較為急
迫，部分專案利潤率優於平均水準，使2Q26 毛利率再次優於
預期。展望 2026 年，隨美國 P2 廠進入主體工程階段，營收
動能將自 2Q26 起加速，加上國內晶圓廠、美國 P3 廠、記憶
體及載板廠接續動工，預估全年營收將逐季走揚。展望後市，
目前在手訂單金額約 380 億元，受惠主要客戶持續加速赴美
擴廠，加上記憶體、載板及矽晶圓產業擴產需求延續，全年新
接單金額有望超越 2025 年，能見度達 1.5~2 年。獲利方面，

Document 158: A Primer on AI Lab & AI Hyperscaler Unit Economics

### Page 1

U.S. Internet
A Primer on AI Lab & AI
Hyperscaler Unit Economics
We expect some confusion as AI labs start reporting GAAP
financials, as business mix and revenue recognition can vary.
Similar to how UBER and LYFT are in the same core business
yet gross bookings and net revenue have different definitions,
comparing AI labs may prove difficult.
The Key Take-Away: The unit economics of AI labs are similar across like-for-like product lines,
but business mix, revenue recognition, and partnerships can lead to fairly significant differences

in how financials are presented. This report attempts to break down these nuances and how
revenue and costs ultimately flow through the P&L at the AI lab. We also look at how the unit
economics of AI labs flow into the unit economics of the supporting hyperscalers.
Business Mix Differences Explain a Lot
AI labs have revenue lines across a number of different products and services. API was the
original AI lab business model, but in recent years applications and subscription products have

entered the mix. Shown below in Figure 1, hypothetical frontier lab "A" has upwards of 70% of
revenue coming from API and 30% from subscription. Hypothetical frontier lab "B" has the
inverse, closer to 80% of revenue from subscription. As we will illustrate further down in this
report, API tends to have higher inference margin than subscription, which in addition to
training costs being completely different, explains most of the difference in gross margins (after

training) at the aggregate AI lab level. It's possible that some of the training costs may be
recorded in R&D for various AI labs, so for the purposes of this report, we are showing ~10% of
training costs in COGS, which represents the final training run for an AI model. It's unclear at this
point how training costs are likely to be allocated across P&L lines by AI labs.
As we will discuss below, other major factors explaining revenue and margin differences are: 1)

AI lab unit economics have improved greatly in '26 (vs. '25) driven by agentic workflows and
enterprise becoming the "must buy" products in the market today. API and enterprise accounts
likely carry higher inference margins; hence, as mix shifts in that direction, and as AI models are
more token efficient per task, inference margins have surged higher. Based on our estimates
shown in Figure 2, we think frontier labs "A" and "B" may have increased paid inference margins
to upwards of 50%-65%+ in '26 (up from mid-teens % in '25), and possibly much higher on
select products and services. After factoring in final training costs (10% of overall training costs),
labs have seen the adjusted gross margin increase ~30-50 points vs. '25. AI lab "B" has strategic
partner revenue sharing which impacts profitability until certain thresholds are achieved, likely
eliminated to zero sometime after '28.

Based on the mix noted above and some of the revenue sharing agreements, training costs, and
inference costs, we conclude that around $35-$40 of every $100 in AI Lab revenue flows to
hyperscaler revenue in '26, on which the hyperscalers generate nearly $10-$20 in OI (at high
~35%-45% operating margin). Partner revenue share results in higher hyperscaler OI margin,
but the actual profit on a per token basis is likely the same excluding these fees. We think there
is a decent possibility that AI lab margins and hyperscalers' OI margins are higher than what we
illustrate here currently in '26, but we would assume these margins come down a bit over time
as competition increases at the frontier and as compute scarcity eases.

AI Products Have a Wide Range of Unit Economics,
and Are Often Recorded Differently
Noted above, the product mix at frontier labs can be dramatically different. Figures 4-6 below
illustrate that even within the same products (like for like) there can be nuances that drive

different unit economics including partnerships (revenue shares) and how actual revenues are
recorded (gross vs. net). Like UBER/LYFT , despite being the same core business, the revenue and
margin can be completely different.
Subscription products (like Claude Code or Codex) generally have lower inference margins than
API as some users benefit from AI labs' willingness to subsidize token cost to remain
competitive and retain users. Subscription products are both monthly-fee based and usage

based, often with caps which can be reset from time to time (we have seen an increase in cap
resets of late, likely in response to churn reduction efforts and model efficiency gains).
Subscription products tend to default to a specific model, but the user can toggle between
models and latency modes for various jobs, which can burn through token credits faster or
slower depending on model size. Enterprise subscriptions are often dollar-based by license

(often at higher limits than Plus and Pro plans), with daily caps that can be allocated across the
licenses. We estimate inference margins on subscriptions are in the 70% range. Noted above, we
would guess that the '26 inference margin may be higher than this level, but over time the
industry may drift back down towards this level to the degree that competitive alternatives
show up and if/when compute moves into surplus (vs. today's shortages).
Hyperscalers generate solid unit economics on agentic subscription products as the compute

being provisioned for these stateful runtime products often involves some "up the stack"
software products like databases, etc., and in some cases have revenue sharing between the AI
lab and the hyperscalers.

Direct API is the original business model for most frontier labs (before even products like
ChatGPT were introduced) and carries very attractive unit economics for both the AI lab and the
hyperscaler. Direct API is where a developer like Cursor or Figma embeds features inside their
products that call on the AI lab's API to generate tokens. The business model for the AI lab is
usage based on token consumption (i.e., pay-as-you-go). Inference margins for API have been
trending up as the models have improved token efficiency (i.e., the volume of tokens to
complete a task) while headline API token prices have increased. Inference providers have
similarly improved the efficiency of serving models at the infrastructure layer (via quantization,
speculators, and next-gen compute), supporting higher hyperscaler margins. Lastly, revenue
sharing between the AI lab and the hyperscaler further boosts the latter's margins (at the
expense of the former).
We have heard that AI lab inference margins on API are running well north of the levels noted in
Fig 5 in 2Q26, but we would guess this may normalize lower at some point (but it's not entirely
clear when).

The Indirect API business model is more or less the same as Direct in terms of what the end user
sees, but billing and the go-to-market relationship is between the user and the hyperscaler
(without direct engagement with the AI lab). AI lab "A" records indirect API revenue on a gross
basis, and it is becoming a larger part of the story. AI lab "B" doesn't have significant revenue
here and records revenue on a net basis, and may exclude revenue recognition entirely when its
strategic partner runs indirect API.
So similar to UBER and LYFT , as indirect grows as a percent of the overall AI lab story, it likely
creates distortion between reported revenues, and hence can distort the narrative around
relative AI lab progress.
28 August 2026 6

Putting It All Together
Right now, just about every dollar of AI lab ARR is finding its way to the hyperscaler revenue line,
because of the high mix of training relative to inference in '26. Over time, inference profits
should eventually surpass training costs, and drive higher profit margins for the AI labs.
Hyperscalers likely start to lose market share of AI lab training and inference starting in '28
when backstopped AI infrastructure projects come online and become the first option for AI
labs. We generally see Amazon Web Services (AWS), Azure (MSFT , covered by Raimo Lenschow),
and Google Cloud Platform (GCP) controlling a similar mix of AI lab compute spend in the next
two years, before this shift to backstopped infrastructure kicks in.

Guide to the Barclays Fundamental Equity Research Rating System:
Our coverage analysts use a relative rating system in which they rate stocks as Overweight, Equal Weight or Underweight (see definitions below)
relative to other companies covered by the analyst or a team of analysts that are deemed to be in the same industry (the "industry coverage universe").

In addition to the stock rating, we provide industry views which rate the outlook for the industry coverage universe as Positive, Neutral or Negative (see
definitions below). A rating system using terms such as buy, hold and sell is not the equivalent of our rating system. Investors should carefully read the
entire research report including the definitions of all ratings and not infer its contents from ratings alone.
Stock Rating
Overweight - The stock is expected to outperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month investment

horizon.
Equal Weight - The stock is expected to perform in line with the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Underweight - The stock is expected to underperform the unweighted expected total return of the industry coverage universe over a 12-month
investment horizon.
Rating Suspended - The rating and target price have been suspended temporarily due to market events that made coverage impracticable or to

comply with applicable regulations and/or firm policies in certain circumstances including where the Investment Bank of Barclays Bank PLC is acting in
an advisory capacity in a merger or strategic transaction involving the company.
Industry View
Positive - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are improving.
Neutral - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are steady, neither improving nor deteriorating.
Negative - industry coverage universe fundamentals/valuations are deteriorating.

Valuation Methodology: We value shares of AMZN using a sum-of-the-parts methodology for its retail and AWS businesses. Our valuation of AMZN's

retail business is based on a 20x EBITDA multiple using a blend of our FY27/28 estimates; while we value AWS using a 6x multiple on a blend of our
FY27/28 revenue estimates. Our resulting price target is $365.
Risks which May Impede the Achievement of the Barclays Research Valuation and Price Target: Downside risks in the short term that could
negatively impact our model and consensus estimates include 1) greater-than-expected slowdown in AWS against the weak macro backdrop, and

hence lower-than-expected OI margins, over a more drawn-out period; 2) further erosion in consumer spending and a persistent input/wage cost
inflation could pressure the Retail business. Longer-term risks include the highly competitive ecommerce landscape, increasing shipping costs from
same and next-day delivery, which could be a drag on margins, security breaches or frequent downtime in the AWS business, which could slow down
public cloud adoption, or execution missteps that could slow growth of the retail business.

### Page 1

J.P. Morgan Securities LLC
Yesterday, we held a virtual NDR for investors, during which we spoke with
Toshiya Hari, Vice President of Investor Relations & Strategic Finance at NVIDIA
Corporation (NVDA). We highlight key takeaways from our conversation below:
• FY28 growth outlook is supported by broad-based improvements in
demand and visibility; NVDA appears comfortable with the 70% Y/Y
growth framework… Mr. Hari indicated that the 70% Y/Y growth framework

for FY28 was not driven by any single factor, but rather by continued strength
across the market, including hyperscalers, neoclouds, AI labs, sovereign AI,
and enterprise/on-prem demand. In addition to having much stronger visibility
into next year, the team noted that its decision to provide an out-year outlook
was also motivated by the fact that there was a meaningful gap between Street
estimates and what NVDA was seeing internally, which the company believed

could have created supply-chain planning challenges for partners. That said,
Mr. Hari noted that 70% Y/Y growth is a figure that NVDA is comfortable with
and that, absent any supply constraints, the business could more than double
Y/Y, reiterating the point that the current outlook remains supply-constrained
rather than demand-constrained.
• Inference is becoming a larger part of NVDA’s DC business, but platform
fungibility makes it difficult to quantify the mix…  One of the recurring

points of discussion throughout our meeting centered on the mix of NVDA’s
training/inferencing revenues, though the team indicated that it was difficult to
precisely size the training versus inference split because NVDA’s platforms are
highly fungible (e.g., customers are able to use Grace Blackwell products for
training and then later shift those same assets toward inference workloads).
Nevertheless, Mr. Hari did indicate that the mix was roughly 50/50 around ~18

months ago and expressed confidence in inference now being larger and
continuing to grow as a percentage of the business over time.
• Advanced wafers and memory are the two most important supply-side
bottlenecks… When discussing NVDA’s ability to meet demand next year and
ongoing supply constraints, Mr. Hari noted that memory and advanced wafers
particularly stood out given their importance within NVDA’s bill of materials
(BOM). Accordingly, the team highlighted that it remains deeply engaged with

TSMC and all three memory vendors (MU, SK Hynix, and Samsung), with
NVDA holding ongoing discussions with each of these suppliers centered
around improving supply availability.
• Customer mix continues to broaden beyond hyperscalers…  Mr. Hari
indicated that the two largest frontier model builders (OpenAI & Anthropic)
should represent ~20% of NVDA’s business today on an end-consumption
basis, with that figure potentially moving toward ~25% in FY28. However,

{[{JVxjt52Wd7w-7azT_vheXCUWyfiV8sQPeo5sSct1_pf8nV26_mCoPLeDvg0_GG7t-d5_Tr_gFeg8qs-GGZ4g-a6W4zFE4g}]}

North America Equity Research
02 September 2026
J P M O R G A N
it is increasingly being driven by a wider ecosystem of customers building and/or leasing
compute capacity.
• Further advancements in computing will require both open-source and closed-
source models, not either/or;  frontier model builders are also driving increasing
gross profit per token as NVIDIA’s compute platform drives strong gen-on-gen cost-

per-token declines... Another recurring topic in our meeting was the debate between
open-source and closed-source LLMs and whether one would become more prominent
going forward. However, consistent with Jensen’s prior comments, Mr. Hari emphasized
that advancements in AI will likely require both open-source and closed-source models
(not either/or). Internally, NVDA uses a broad range of closed models such as OpenAI
and Claude, while for mission-critical workloads like chip design, the company relies on

a blend of closed and open-source approaches. In any case, as long as model builders are
able to monetize their products and continue to improve their economics, demand should
flow through to chip providers such as NVDA. The team also notably mentioned that
model-builder GMs appear to be improving, likely supported by NVDA’s ability to drive
gen-on-gen cost-per-token improvements.
• NVDA’s financing vehicles are meant to support stronger forward demand for AI

infrastructure… Mr. Hari also walked through several of NVDA’s financing vehicles,
including the company’s revenue-share agreements with select neoclouds, the PORTS-
Pike DC campus initiative, and the $500B private capital financing platform with Apollo,
BlackRock, Blackstone, Brookfield, Goldman Sachs, KKR, and others. On the revenue-
share agreements, NVDA explained that it helps backstop a base-price and shares in the
upside if compute is leased at higher market rates (which creates recurring revenue

optionality on top of core hardware sales). More broadly, the NVDA team continues to
push back on circular financing concerns, as it believes its financing agreements are
measured, capped, and supported by strong underlying demand, ecosystem returns, and
the credit profile of the ultimate compute purchasers.

Investment Thesis, Valuation and Risks
NVIDIA Corporation (Overweight; Price Target: $320.00)
Investment Thesis
NVIDIA's vertically integrated platform (spanning multiple chips, rack systems, and the
software stack that ties them together) is difficult to replicate and represents a competitive
advantage relative to AI compute peers.  Multi-year demand visibility remains exceptionally

strong and is explicitly framed as a floor, excluding several incremental revenue streams
while being layered on top of a structural, underappreciated shift of traditional enterprise
workloads to accelerated compute that is independent of the AI training/inference cycle.
We see constructive demand fundamentals coupled with an inflecting capital return
supporting material upside to the stock.
Valuation
Our PT assumes NVDA trades at ~20x (reflecting long-term growth rate expectations)

applied to CY26 EPS of $15.87.
Risks to Rating and Price Target
• TAM share capture by alternative compute platforms (AI ASICs/XPUs primarily, but
also merchant competitors (AMD) and specialized accelerators) has been an ongoing
narrative headwind, and one that is challenging to dispel entirely given the significant
program ramps planned over the next several years; we expect GPU and ASIC/XPU
share of the AI compute TAM to trend toward parity in coming years.

• Memory cost inflation and supply constraints have pressured platform specifications
and raise questions over the longer-term durability of gross margins, introducing
uncertainty around both top-line mix and profitability as the company scales back
memory capacity to meet compute volume commitments.

Document 312: 【國泰證期研究部】半導體測試產業-Insertion 2啟動，雙面光電測試價值上移 -20260831

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】半導體測試產業-Insertion 2啟動，雙面光電測試價值上移 -20260831.pdf
